# Get transactions from the last 8 weeks excluding current week
# First, calculate the start of the current week (Monday)
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Calculate 8 weeks ago from the start of current week
eight_weeks_ago = DATETIME(current_week_start, '-56 days')

# Filter transactions for the last 8 weeks excluding current week
recent_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (date_time >= eight_weeks_ago) & 
    (date_time < current_week_start)
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate week start (Monday) for each transaction
    week_start = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    # Check if transaction occurred on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1 in DAYOFWEEK)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(date_time), (1, 7))
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = recent_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.ASC())

WITH _t3 AS (
  SELECT
    sbtxdatetime AS date_time
  FROM main.sbtransaction
  WHERE
    sbtxdatetime < DATE(
      'now',
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
    AND sbtxdatetime >= DATETIME(
      DATE(
        'now',
        '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
        'start of day'
      ),
      '-56 day'
    )
), _s0 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT() AS agg_0,
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    ) AS week_start
  FROM _t3
  GROUP BY
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
), _s1 AS (
  SELECT
    SUM(IIF(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', date_time) AS INTEGER) IN (1, 7), 1, 0)) AS agg_1,
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    ) AS week_start
  FROM _t3
  GROUP BY
    DATE(
      date_time,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(date_time)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
)
SELECT
  _s0.week_start,
  COALESCE(_s0.agg_0, 0) AS total_transactions,
  COALESCE(_s1.agg_1, 0) AS weekend_transactions
FROM _s0 AS _s0
LEFT JOIN _s1 AS _s1
  ON _s0.week_start = _s1.week_start
ORDER BY
  week_start

start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_window = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')

# Filter for stock transactions within the specified date range
recent_stock_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_window) &
    (date_time < start_of_current_week)
)

# Calculate the start of the week for each transaction and identify if it was a weekend
transaction_info = recent_stock_transactions.CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Group transactions by the start of the week and count total and weekend transactions
result = transaction_info.PARTITION(name='weekly_groups', by=(week_start_date)).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=week_start_date,
    total_transactions=COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions=SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start_date.DESC())

)

# First, filter customers who joined in the last 6 months excluding current month
# We need to calculate the date range
customers_last_6_months = Customers.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) <= 6) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", join_date, DATETIME("now")) >= 1)
).CALCULATE(
    join_month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, "0"))
)

# Partition by join month to calculate PMCS
monthly_stats = customers_last_6_months.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=join_month
).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers),
    pmat = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
)

result = monthly_stats.ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol,
    amount=amount,
    tax=tax,
    commission=commission
)

    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount=SUM(filtered_sells.amount),
    total_costs=SUM(filtered_sells.tax) + SUM(filtered_sells.commission)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    SPM=IFF(total_amount > 0, (total_amount - total_costs) / total_amount * 100.0, 0.0)
)

# Step 1: Extract month and symbol from daily prices
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    year = YEAR(date),
    month_num = MONTH(date)
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(
    name="symbol_month", 
    by=(symbol, month)
).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    year = year,
    month_num = month_num,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV to get previous month's average
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    prev_avg_close = PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker"),
    momc = (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), year.ASC(), month_num.ASC()), per="Broker")
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q13] Code generated by gemini:
# Step 1: Prepare daily price data with symbol and a sortable month string (YYYY-MM)
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition by ticker and month to calculate monthly aggregates
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="ticker_months", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the aggregated monthly stats by symbol to facilitate the MoM calculation
# This creates groups where each group contains all monthly data for a single ticker
ticker_groups = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="tickers", by=symbol)

# Step 4: Within each ticker's group of months, calculate the MoMC
# Use the PREV window function to get the prior month's average closing price
result = ticker_groups.monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    # Get the previous month's average close, ordered by month, for each ticker
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="tickers")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    avg_close=avg_close,
    max_high=max_high,
    min_low=min_low,
    # Calculate MoMC, handling cases where the previous month's data is null or zero
    momc=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close == None) | (prev_avg_close == 0),
        None,
        (avg_close - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close
    )
)

    customer_state = customer.state,
    ticker_type = ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition by the combination of customer state and ticker type
state_type_groups = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_combos", 
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(

    ticker_type = ticker_type,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Filter transactions from the past 30 days (inclusive)
recent_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 30
)

# Add country information to each transaction
transactions_with_country = recent_transactions.CALCULATE(
    country = customer.country,
    amount = amount
)

# Group by country and calculate aggregates
country_stats = transactions_with_country.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country = country,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    total_amount = SUM(Transactions.amount)
)

# Get top 5 countries by total amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_amount.DESC())

q1_2023_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(status) == 'success') & 
    (YEAR(date_time) == 2023) & 
    (MONTH(date_time) >= 1) & 
    (MONTH(date_time) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(

    month = MONTH(date_time),
    price = price
)

# Partition by year and month, then calculate average price

    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(year, month)
).CALCULATE(
    year = year,
    month = month,
    avg_price = AVG(Transactions.price)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Get sales with sale price over $30,000 and their payments
high_value_sales_payments = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    # Calculate the week start date (Monday) for each payment
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    # Determine if payment was on weekend (Saturday=6, Sunday=0 in week starting Monday)
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date), (6, 7))
)

# Filter to last 8 weeks excluding current week
# Current week start
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
# 8 weeks ago from the start of current week
eight_weeks_ago = DATETIME(current_week_start, '-56 days')

filtered_payments = high_value_sales_payments.WHERE(
    (week_start >= eight_weeks_ago) & (week_start < current_week_start)
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = filtered_payments.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the 8-week analysis period.
start_of_current_week = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')

# Filter sales over $30,000, access their payments, and filter for the last 8 weeks.
# Also, calculate the week start date and a weekend flag for each payment.
payments_in_window = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_period) & (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(

    is_weekend=IFF(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date) >= 6, 1, 0)
)

    name="weekly_groups", by=(week_start_date,)

    week=week_start_date,

# First, get cars that have been sold and are not in inventory
sold_cars_not_in_inventory = Cars.WHERE(
    # Car must have been sold
    HAS(sale_records) == 1
).WHERE(
    # Check the most recent inventory snapshot shows it's not in inventory
    inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars", allow_ties=False) == 1
    ).SINGULAR().is_in_inventory == False
)

# Now get the sale information for these cars

    car_make=make,
    car_model=model,
    max_sale_price=MAX(sale_records.sale_price)
)

# Group by make and model to get the highest sale price for each combination
result = cars_with_sales.PARTITION(
    name="make_model_groups", 
    by=(car_make, car_model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=car_make,
    model=car_model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.sale_records.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

# Define a contextless expression to efficiently get the latest inventory snapshot for each car.
# This is done by ranking the snapshots by date in descending order for each car and picking the top one.
# SINGULAR() is used to treat the result as a single record, allowing direct field access.
latest_inventory_status = Cars.inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
    RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars") == 1
).SINGULAR()

# Start with the Cars collection and filter for cars that meet two criteria:
# 1. The car has at least one sale record (HAS(sale_records) == 1).
# 2. The car's most recent inventory status is 'not in inventory' (is_in_inventory == False).
sold_and_out_of_stock_cars = Cars.WHERE(
    (HAS(sale_records) == 1) & (latest_inventory_status.is_in_inventory == False)
)

# Partition the filtered cars by make and model to group them.
# For each group, calculate the make, model, and the maximum sale price from all associated sale records.
# Finally, order the results by the highest sale price in descending order.
highest_sales_by_make_model = sold_and_out_of_stock_cars.PARTITION(
    name="car_groups", by=(make, model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=make,
    model=model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.sale_records.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
six_months_ago = DATETIME(current_month_start, '-6 months')

# Filter salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023
eligible_salespersons = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
)

# Get all sales from eligible salespersons with month truncation
sales_with_month = eligible_salespersons.sales_made.CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson.salesperson_id,
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price=sale_price
)

    (sale_month >= six_months_ago) & (sale_month < current_month_start)
)

result = sales_in_range.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=(salesperson_id, sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    month=sale_month,
    pmsps=COUNT(sales_made),  # Per month salesperson sales count
    pmsr=SUM(sales_made.sale_price)  # Per month sales revenue

# Define the date boundaries for the last 6 months, excluding the current month.
date_boundaries = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Filter sales to the specified date range and for salespersons hired in 2022-2023.
# Also, create a 'sale_month' column for grouping.
sales_to_group = date_boundaries.Sales.WHERE(
    (sale_date >= start_date) &
    (sale_date < end_date) &
    (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) &
    (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
).CALCULATE(
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered sales by month to calculate the required metrics.
# The result is ordered by month in ascending order.
monthly_summary = sales_to_group.PARTITION(
    name="sales_by_month",
    by=(sale_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month,
    PMSPS=COUNT(Sales),
    PMSR=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, for each sale that has associated payments, we calculate the average number of days
# from the sale date to the payment date. This is done by calculating the day difference
# for each payment and then averaging those differences per sale.
per_sale_avg_days = Sales.WHERE(HAS(payment) == 1).CALCULATE(
    avg_days_to_pay_for_sale=AVG(
        payment.CALCULATE(
            day_diff=DATEDIFF("days", sale_date, payment_date)
        ).day_diff
    )
)

# Next, we calculate the overall average of these per-sale averages.
# This gives a single value representing the average payment time across all sales,
# which is then rounded to two decimal places as requested.
average_payment_time = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    average_days_to_payment=ROUND(AVG(per_sale_avg_days.avg_days_to_pay_for_sale), 2)
)

)

    year,
    month,

    prev_month_total = PREV(
        total_payments,

    ),
    mom_change = total_payments - PREV(
        total_payments, 
        by=(year.ASC(), month.ASC()),
        default=0
    )
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC(), month.ASC())

# Get payments from the previous ISO week
previous_week_payments = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')) &
    (payment_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
)

# Add date and payment method to each payment
payments_with_info = previous_week_payments.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    payment_amount=payment_amount
)

# Partition by date and payment method to group the payments
daily_method_groups = payments_with_info.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
)

# Calculate the total for each date and payment method combination
result = daily_method_groups.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    daily_total=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

# Define the start of the current week and the start of the previous week.
# The previous week is the 7-day period before the start of the current week.
date_info = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_previous_week=DATETIME(DATETIME('now', 'start of week'), '-7 days')
)

# Filter payments to include only those from the previous week.
payments_in_previous_week = date_info.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_previous_week) & (payment_date < start_of_current_week)
)

# Partition the filtered payments by date and method, then calculate the sum for each group.
daily_payment_totals = payments_in_previous_week.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups",
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_day=payment_date,
    method=payment_method,
    total_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)

# First, prepare sales data with year, quarter, and customer state
sales_2023 = Sales.WHERE(YEAR(sale_date) == 2023).CALCULATE(
    sale_year = YEAR(sale_date),
    sale_quarter = IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 3, 1,
                      IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 6, 2,
                          IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 9, 3, 4))),
    customer_state = customer.state,
    sale_price = sale_price
)

# Partition by state and quarter to calculate totals
quarterly_sales = sales_2023.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, sale_quarter)
).CALCULATE(
    state = customer_state,
    quarter = sale_quarter,
    quarter_start_date = IFF(sale_quarter == 1, "2023-01-01",
                            IFF(sale_quarter == 2, "2023-04-01",
                                IFF(sale_quarter == 3, "2023-07-01", "2023-10-01"))),
    total_sales = SUM(Sales.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(state.ASC(), quarter.ASC())

result = quarterly_sales

# For each sale, calculate the customer's state and the first date of the quarter.
# A nested IFF statement is used to map the month to the correct quarter start date.
sales_with_quarter_info = sales_in_2023.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    quarter_date=IFF(
        MONTH(sale_date) <= 3,
        DATETIME('2023-01-01'),
        IFF(
            MONTH(sale_date) <= 6,
            DATETIME('2023-04-01'),
            IFF(
                MONTH(sale_date) <= 9,
                DATETIME('2023-07-01'),
                DATETIME('2023-10-01')
            )
        )
    )
)

# Partition the data by state and quarter, then calculate the total sales for each group.
quarterly_sales_by_state = sales_with_quarter_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups",
    by=(customer_state, quarter_date)
).CALCULATE(
    state=customer_state,
    quarter=quarter_date,
    total_quarterly_sales=SUM(Sales.sale_price)
)

# First, calculate the date range for the last 12 months excluding current month
# We'll filter treatments that started within this range
treatments_with_month = Treatments.WHERE(
    # Filter for treatments in the last 12 months, excluding current month
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) > 0) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 12)
).CALCULATE(
    # Extract month in yyyy-mm format
    month=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(start_dt), LPAD(MONTH(start_dt), 2, "0")),
    patient_id=patient_id
)

filtered_treatments = DermTreatment.CALCULATE(
    start_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-12 months'),
    end_date=DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
).Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_date) & (start_dt < end_date)
)

# Step 1: For each patient with treatments, find their first treatment year
patients_with_first_year = Patients.WHERE(
    HAS(treatments_received) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    patient_id,
    first_treatment_year=YEAR(MIN(treatments_received.start_dt))
)

# Step 2: Group by year and count new patients
yearly_new_patients = patients_with_first_year.PARTITION(
    name="years", 
    by=first_treatment_year
).CALCULATE(
    year=first_treatment_year,
    num_new_patients=COUNT(Patients)
)

# Step 3: Calculate NPI (increase from previous year)
result = yearly_new_patients.CALCULATE(
    year,
    num_new_patients,
    npi=num_new_patients - PREV(num_new_patients, by=year.ASC(), default=0)
).ORDER_BY(year.ASC())

# First, let's get treatments with their adverse events that occurred in the same month
treatments_with_same_month_ae = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    treatment_month=MONTH(start_dt),
    treatment_year=YEAR(start_dt),
    same_month_ae_count=COUNT(
        adverse_events.WHERE(
            (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt)) & 
            (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt))
        )
    )
).WHERE(same_month_ae_count > 0)

# Now group by drug and sum up the adverse events
drug_ae_counts = treatments_with_same_month_ae.PARTITION(
    name="drug_groups", 
    by=drug_id
).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    total_ae_count=SUM(treatments_with_same_month_ae.same_month_ae_count)
)

# Join with drug information and find the top drug
result = drug_ae_counts.TOP_K(1, by=total_ae_count.DESC()).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=Drugs.WHERE(drug_id == drug_id).SINGULAR().drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=total_ae_count
)

    DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME("now")) <= 6

    drug_amount=tot_drug_amt

    name="specialties", 
    by=doctor_specialty
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doctor_specialty,
    num_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
)

# Get top 3 specialties by total drug amount
result = specialty_stats.TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

# First, filter treatments to include only those started in the last 6 months
# and calculate the doctor's specialty, which will be used for grouping.
recent_treatments_with_specialty = DermTreatment.Treatments.WHERE(
    start_dt >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months')
).CALCULATE(
    doc_specialty=doctor.speciality
)

# Next, partition the filtered treatments by the doctor's specialty.
# For each specialty, calculate the total number of treatments and the sum of the total drug amount.
# Finally, select the top 3 specialties based on the total drug amount in descending order.
top_specialties_by_drug_amount = recent_treatments_with_specialty.PARTITION(
    name="specialty_groups", by=(doc_specialty)
).CALCULATE(
    specialty=doc_specialty,
    number_of_treatments=COUNT(Treatments),
    total_drug_amount=SUM(Treatments.tot_drug_amt)
).TOP_K(3, by=total_drug_amount.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q94] Code generated by gemini:
merchant_cpur = Ewallet.Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    # First, we isolate the successful transactions for each merchant.
    # A transaction is successful if its status is 'success'.
    successful_transactions=transactions_received.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    # Now, we calculate the CPUR using the successful transactions.
    # We use IFF to handle cases where a merchant has no successful transactions, preventing division by zero.
    CPUR=IFF(
        NDISTINCT(successful_transactions.txid) > 0,
        NDISTINCT(successful_transactions.coupon_id) / NDISTINCT(successful_transactions.txid),
        0.0
    )
)

# Get notifications from US and Canada users for the last 3 weeks excluding current week
us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    (LOWER(country) == LOWER('US')) | (LOWER(country) == LOWER('Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 weeks excluding current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(
    # Truncate created_at to start of week
    week_start = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    # Check if notification was sent on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(created_at), (1, 7))
)

# Partition by week and calculate totals
result = us_canada_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(Notifications.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.DESC())

# Define the time boundaries for the analysis: the start of the current week and the start of the 3-week period prior.
time_boundaries = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications to include only those from users in the US or Canada and within the specified time frame.
# Also, calculate the start of the week for each notification and a flag indicating if it was sent on a weekend.
filtered_notifications = time_boundaries.Users.WHERE(
    ISIN(country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) & (created_at < start_of_current_week)
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Group the filtered notifications by the week they were sent.
# For each week, count the total number of notifications and the number of weekend notifications.
weekly_notification_summary = filtered_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

# Assign the final result to the output variable.
result = weekly_notification_summary

# The date filter DATEDIFF("days", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 6 correctly captures the last 7 days, including today.

)

# We need to filter transactions where:
# 1. sender_type = 0
# 2. created_at is in the last 2 months but not in the current month

# First, let's calculate the date range
# Current month start would be DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
# 2 months ago would be DATETIME('now', '-2 months', 'start of month')

transactions_filtered = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) & 
    (created_at >= DATETIME('now', '-2 months', 'start of month')) &
    (created_at < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(

    month=JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(created_at), LPAD(MONTH(created_at), 2, '0'))
)

    name='monthly_groups', 
    by=month
).CALCULATE(
    month=month,
    pmdau=NDISTINCT(monthly_groups.sender_id)

# Define the time window: the start of two months ago to the start of the current month.
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
end_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions to the relevant time window and user type.
# Also, pre-calculate date components (year, month, day) for partitioning.
transactions_in_period = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) &
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < end_of_period)
).CALCULATE(
    year=YEAR(created_at),
    month=MONTH(created_at),
    day=DAY(created_at),
    sender_id=sender_id
)

# First, partition by day to calculate the Daily Active Users (DAU).
# DAU is the number of distinct senders for each day.
daily_active_users = transactions_in_period.PARTITION(
    name="daily_groups",
    by=(year, month, day)
).CALCULATE(
    year=year,
    month=month,
    dau_count=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
)

# Next, partition the daily results by month to calculate the average DAU for each month (PMDAU).
pmdau_by_month = daily_active_users.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups",
    by=(year, month)
).CALCULATE(
    # Format the month string as 'yyyy-mm'.
    month_formatted=JOIN_STRINGS('-', year, LPAD(month, 2, '0')),
    # Calculate PMDAU by averaging the daily counts within the month.
    pmdau=AVG(daily_groups.dau_count)
)

# Select the final fields for the output.
pmdau_result = pmdau_by_month.CALCULATE(
    month=month_formatted,
    pmdau=pmdau
)

# Get transactions from the previous month and calculate STR
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    # Get the start of the previous month
    prev_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month'),
    # Get the start of the current month (end of previous month)
    curr_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
).CALCULATE(
    # Filter transactions from the previous month
    prev_month_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & (created_at < curr_month_start)
    ),
    # Count successful transactions (case insensitive comparison)
    successful_transactions = COUNT(Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & 
        (created_at < curr_month_start) & 
        (LOWER(status) == 'success')
    )),
    # Count total transactions
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & (created_at < curr_month_start)
    ))
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate STR as a percentage
    str_percentage = IFF(
        total_transactions > 0,
        100.0 * successful_transactions / total_transactions,
        0.0
    ),
    successful_transactions = successful_transactions,
    total_transactions = total_transactions
)

start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_previous_month = DATETIME(start_of_current_month, '-1 month')

# Filter transactions to include only those from the previous month
transactions_in_last_month = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
)

# Calculate the number of successful and total transactions
transaction_counts = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    total_transactions=COUNT(transactions_in_last_month)
)

# Calculate the Success Transaction Rate (STR) and select only that field
wallet_str_previous_month = transaction_counts.CALCULATE(
    str=IFF(total_transactions > 0, successful_transactions / total_transactions, 0.0)
)

merchant_coupon_counts = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_mid=mid,
    merchant_name=name,
    merchant_year=YEAR(created_at),
    merchant_month=MONTH(created_at),
    same_month_coupon_count=COUNT(
        coupons.WHERE(
            (YEAR(created_at) == YEAR(merchant.created_at)) & 
            (MONTH(created_at) == MONTH(merchant.created_at))
        )
    )
)

    mid=merchant_mid,
    name=merchant_name,

)

# First, filter transactions to include only those made by active users in 2023 that were successful.
# A 'transaction_month' field is created by truncating the creation date to the start of the month.
transactions_in_2023 = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (LOWER(status) == 'completed') &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &
    (LOWER(sending_user.status) == 'active')
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition the filtered transactions by month.
# For each month, count the number of distinct users who sent money.
monthly_active_senders = transactions_in_2023.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_transactions",
    by=(transaction_month)
).CALCULATE(
    month=transaction_month,
    active_users_count=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sending_user.uid)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# First, filter active users and their successful transactions in 2023
active_users_transactions_2023 = Users.WHERE(
    LOWER(status) == LOWER('active')
).transactions_sent.WHERE(
    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) & 
    (LOWER(status) == LOWER('successful')) &
    (sender_type == 1)  # Assuming 1 is for users, 0 for merchants based on the int32 type
).CALCULATE(
    user_id=sender_id,
    month_date=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')
)

# Partition by month and count distinct users
monthly_active_users = active_users_transactions_2023.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=month_date
).CALCULATE(
    month=month_date,
    active_users=NDISTINCT(active_users_transactions_2023.user_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

result = monthly_active_users

# First, let's get users with their country and filter for those who sent transactions with sender_type = 0
users_with_transactions = Users.WHERE(
    HAS(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0)) == 1
).CALCULATE(
    user_country = country,
    user_id = uid,
    total_sent = SUM(transactions_sent.WHERE(sender_type == 0).amount)
)

# Now partition by country to aggregate the data
country_stats = users_with_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=user_country
).CALCULATE(
    country = user_country,
    num_distinct_users = COUNT(Users),
    total_transaction_amount = SUM(Users.total_sent)
)

# Get the top 5 countries by total transaction amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_transaction_amount.DESC())

# Get today's date
today_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of day')

# Filter active merchants with retail category and get their today's balance
active_retail_merchants = Merchants.WHERE(
    (LOWER(status) == 'active') & 
    (CONTAINS(LOWER(category), 'retail'))
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    # Get the most recent balance for today
    today_balance=balances.WHERE(
        updated_at >= today_start
    ).TOP_K(1, by=updated_at.DESC()).SINGULAR().balance
)

# Calculate the median balance
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    median_balance=MEDIAN(active_retail_merchants.today_balance)

# For each merchant, find their earliest coupon and check if it's within a year of registration
result = Merchants.WHERE(HAS(coupons) == 1).CALCULATE(
    merchant_id=mid,
    registration_date=created_at,
    # Find the earliest coupon for this merchant
    earliest_coupon_id=coupons.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().cid,
    earliest_coupon_start_date=coupons.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=start_date.ASC(), per="Merchants") == 1
    ).SINGULAR().start_date
).WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", registration_date, earliest_coupon_start_date) <= 365
)
